The locale fixes an effectful_domain_transfer and assumes the three TD_side preconditions on side_cfg_T_eff directly. |
| locale | `td_cfg_side_solver_eff` | 20 | locale td_cfg_side_solver_eff =<br>  fixes g :: cfg and<br>  etf :: "('g::finite, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer" and<br>  bot0 s0 :: "'a abs_state" and gseed :: 'g<br>  assumes mono_eq:    "is_mono_eq (side_cfg_T_eff g etf bot0 s0 gseed)"<br>    and   mono_sides: "mono_sides (side_cfg_T_eff g etf bot0 s0 gseed)"<br>    and   mono_deps:  "mono_deps (side_cfg_T_eff g etf bot0 s0 gseed)"<br>begin |  |
| definition | `cfg_pkg_eff` | 29 | definition cfg_pkg_eff :: "(pp, 'g, 'a abs_state) eqsT"<br>  where "cfg_pkg_eff = side_cfg_T_eff g etf bot0 s0 gseed" |  |
| definition | `stabl_at` | 42 | definition stabl_at :: "pp ⇒ pp set"<br>  where "stabl_at x = fst (side.solve x)" |  |
| definition | `nu_at` | 45 | definition nu_at :: "pp ⇒ pp + 'g ⇒ 'a abs_state"<br>  where "nu_at x = snd (side.solve x)" |  |
| definition | `env_at` | 48 | definition env_at :: "pp ⇒ pp ⇒ 'a abs_state"<br>  where "env_at x0 v = side_env (nu_at x0) v" |  |
| definition | `side_analyse_eff` | 82 | definition side_analyse_eff ::<br>    "proc_table ⇒ pname list ⇒ pname ⇒ com<br>     ⇒ ('g::finite, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer<br>     ⇒ 'a abs_state ⇒ 'a abs_state ⇒ 'g ⇒ pp ⇒ 'a abs_state"<br>where<br>  "side_analyse_eff Π ps mnm main etf bot0 s0 gseed v =<br>     side_env (td_cfg_side_solver_eff.nu_at (compile_prog Π ps mnm main) etf bot0 s0 gseed v) v"<br><br>end | Executable-facing combined env at each pp (entry query). |

### src/Analysis/Generic/Solver/Core/TD_Side_Eff_Pipeline.thy

> The pipeline derives the solver interface from monotonicity and static dependencies of each strategy tree. A partial post-solution bounds every contribution, and a sound effectful transfer turns those bounds into collecting-semantics soundness. Effectful analyses discharge the tree contracts compositionally.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| definition | `threefold_mono` | 19 | definition threefold_mono ::<br>  "('x, 'g, 'd::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) eqsT ⇒ bool"<br>where<br>  "threefold_mono T ≡ is_mono_eq T ∧ mono_sides T ∧ mono_deps T" | `threefold_mono` bundles the conditions required by the optimized solver: equation values and side effects are monotone in the environment, while dependency sets can only shrink as the environment grows. Together they guarantee a least partial post-solution. |
| definition | `cone_compatible_etf` | 86 | definition cone_compatible_etf ::<br>  "('g::finite, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer ⇒ bool"<br>where<br>  "cone_compatible_etf etf ≡<br>     (∀b z σ'. Inl z ∈ dep_aux σ' (apply_etf etf b z)) ∧<br>     (∀c2 e2 d2 σ'. Inl c2 ∈ dep_aux σ' (etf_combine etf d2 c2 e2)) ∧<br>     (∀c2 e2 d2 σ'. Inl e2 ∈ dep_aux σ' (etf_combine etf d2 c2 e2)) ∧<br>     (∀cl fs as σ'. Inl cl ∈ dep_aux σ' (etf_enter etf fs as cl)) ∧<br>     (∀a u. static_deps (apply_etf etf a u)) ∧<br>     (∀cc ex dst. static_deps (etf_combine etf dst cc ex)) ∧<br>     (∀cl fs as. static_deps (etf_enter etf fs as cl)) ∧<br>     (∀a u σ' g. local_bot_on_locals (sides_of_rhs (apply_etf etf a u) σ' (Inr g))) ∧<br>     (∀cc ex dst σ' g. local_bot_on_locals (sides_of_rhs (etf_combine etf dst cc ex) σ' (Inr g))) ∧<br>     (∀cl fs as σ' g. local_bot_on_locals (sides_of_rhs (etf_enter etf fs as cl) σ' (Inr g)))" | cone_compatible_etf bundles the five structural conditions that guarantee the solver's stable set reaches the program entry (the cone argument in side_cone_in_vars_eff). The first three are self-reference conditions: each edge/combine tree queries its own source program points. The last two say the queried unknowns are independent of the environment (static_deps), so the cone is determined structurally rather than by the abstract state. |

### src/Analysis/Generic/Solver/Core/TD_Side_RHS_Generator.thy

> `unit_etf_of_transfer` and `mixed_etf_of_transfer` share combine-tree shape and differ only in per-edge tree dispatch. These locales bundle the transfer-tree shape, dependency-staticness facts, and solver-side monotonicity packaging for each shape.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| locale | `sound_rhs_generator_base` | 16 | locale sound_rhs_generator_base =<br>  fixes etf :: "(unit, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer"<br>  assumes comb: "⋀cc ex dst. etf_combine etf dst cc ex = unit_combine_tree dst cc ex"<br>begin |  |
| locale | `sound_rhs_generator_static` | 35 | locale sound_rhs_generator_static = sound_rhs_generator_base<br>begin |  |
| locale | `mixed_rhs_generator` | 66 | locale mixed_rhs_generator = sound_rhs_generator_static +<br>  fixes F :: "edge_action ⇒ 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot abs_state ⇒ 'a abs_state"<br>    and Fe :: "vname list ⇒ aexp list ⇒ 'a abs_state ⇒ 'a abs_state"<br>  assumes edge: "⋀a u. apply_etf etf a u = local_edge_tree (F a) u<br>                       ∨ apply_etf etf a u = unit_edge_tree (F a) u"<br>  assumes enter: "⋀cl fs as. etf_enter etf fs as cl = unit_edge_tree (Fe fs as) cl"<br>begin | Each per-edge tree is a local or a unit edge tree. The disjunction form subsumes both the pure-unit generator (always the unit disjunct) and a genuine local/unit mix, so one locale covers both. |
| locale | `mixed_rhs_generator_mono` | 112 | locale mixed_rhs_generator_mono = mixed_rhs_generator +<br>  assumes F_mono: "⋀a s1 s2. s1 ≤ s2 ⟹ F a s1 ≤ F a s2"<br>    and Fe_mono: "⋀fs as s1 s2. s1 ≤ s2 ⟹ Fe fs as s1 ≤ Fe fs as s2"<br>begin |  |

### src/Analysis/Generic/Solver/Core/TD_Side_Tree.thy

> Each equation right-hand side folds three contribution families: ordinary CFG edges, procedure-entry transfers, and return/combine transfers. The accumulator joins their local results, while each strategy tree may emit side contributions to named global slots. A return/combine tree joins caller locals with callee-exit globals. Its local result flows to the resume point, and its global result flows through side effects.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| fun | `fold_rhs_trees` | 27 | fun fold_rhs_trees ::<br>  "'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot<br>   ⇒ ('k, 'g, 'a) strategy_tree list<br>   ⇒ ('k, 'g, 'a) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "fold_rhs_trees acc [] = Answer acc"<br>\| "fold_rhs_trees acc (t # ts) =<br>     seqcomp_tree t (λres. fold_rhs_trees (acc ⊔ res) ts)" | The generic fold composes a list of contribution trees with `seqcomp_tree`. `side_contribution_trees` assembles the three source families in their executable order; both construction and denotation use that same list. |
| definition | `side_contribution_trees` | 36 | definition side_contribution_trees ::<br>  "('g, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer<br>   ⇒ (pp × edge_action) list<br>   ⇒ (pp × vname list × aexp list) list<br>   ⇒ (pp × vname option × pp) list<br>   ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'a abs_state) strategy_tree list"<br>where<br>  "side_contribution_trees etf es ens cs =<br>     map (λ(u, a). apply_etf etf a u) es @<br>     map (λ(cl, fs, as). etf_enter etf fs as cl) ens @<br>     map (λ(cc, dst, ex). etf_combine etf dst cc ex) cs" |  |
| definition | `side_rhs_fold_eff` | 48 | definition side_rhs_fold_eff ::<br>  "('g, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer<br>   ⇒ 'a abs_state<br>   ⇒ (pp × edge_action) list<br>   ⇒ (pp × vname list × aexp list) list<br>   ⇒ (pp × vname option × pp) list<br>   ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'a abs_state) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "side_rhs_fold_eff etf acc es ens cs =<br>     fold_rhs_trees acc (side_contribution_trees etf es ens cs)" |  |
| definition | `entry_seed_list` | 87 | definition entry_seed_list :: "cfg ⇒ pp ⇒ (pp × vname list × aexp list) list" where<br>  "entry_seed_list g v =<br>     map (λ(c, ca). case ca of CallEdge dst fs as ⇒ (c, fs, as)) (entry_call_list g v)" | The callee-entry seed list: each incoming call at callee entry `v` contributes its formals/actuals so the fold can invoke `etf_enter` on the caller state. |
| definition | `make_side_rhs_tree_eff` | 91 | definition make_side_rhs_tree_eff ::<br>  "cfg ⇒ ('g, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer<br>   ⇒ 'a abs_state ⇒ 'a abs_state ⇒ 'g ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'a abs_state) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "make_side_rhs_tree_eff g etf bot0 s0 gseed v =<br>     (let acc0 = (if v = cfg_entry g then bot0 ⊔ restrict_local s0 else bot0);<br>          t    = side_rhs_fold_eff etf acc0<br>                   (intra_predecessor_list g v) (entry_seed_list g v)<br>                   (return_call_list g v)<br>      in if v = cfg_entry g then Side gseed (restrict_global s0) t else t)" |  |
| definition | `side_cfg_T_eff` | 103 | definition side_cfg_T_eff ::<br>  "cfg ⇒ ('g, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer<br>   ⇒ 'a abs_state ⇒ 'a abs_state ⇒ 'g<br>   ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'a abs_state) eqsT"<br>where<br>  "side_cfg_T_eff g etf bot0 s0 gseed = make_side_rhs_tree_eff g etf bot0 s0 gseed" |  |
| fun | `fold_rhs_values` | 112 | fun fold_rhs_values ::<br>  "'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot<br>   ⇒ ('k + 'g ⇒ 'a)<br>   ⇒ ('k, 'g, 'a) strategy_tree list<br>   ⇒ 'a"<br>where<br>  "fold_rhs_values acc σ [] = acc"<br>\| "fold_rhs_values acc σ (t # ts) =<br>     fold_rhs_values (acc ⊔ traverse_rhs t σ) σ ts" |  |
| definition | `side_acc_eff` | 122 | definition side_acc_eff ::<br>  "('g, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer<br>   ⇒ 'a abs_state<br>   ⇒ (pp + 'g ⇒ 'a abs_state)<br>   ⇒ (pp × edge_action) list<br>   ⇒ (pp × vname list × aexp list) list<br>   ⇒ (pp × vname option × pp) list ⇒ 'a abs_state"<br>where<br>  "side_acc_eff etf acc σ es ens cs =<br>     fold_rhs_values acc σ (side_contribution_trees etf es ens cs)" |  |
| definition | `edge_constraint_tree` | 181 | definition edge_constraint_tree ::<br>  "('g, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer<br>   ⇒ pp ⇒ edge_action ⇒ pp ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'a abs_state) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "edge_constraint_tree etf u a v = apply_etf etf a u" | Each incoming CFG edge contributes the transfer of its source unknown to the target equation. The transfer depends on the source point and edge action; the target point selects the equation that receives the contribution. `edge_constraint_tree` exposes this contribution as a strategy tree. |
| primrec | `map_ltree` | 404 | primrec map_ltree ::<br>  "('x ⇒ 'y) ⇒ ('x, 'g, 'd) strategy_tree ⇒ ('y, 'g, 'd) strategy_tree" where<br>  "map_ltree h (Answer d) = Answer d"<br>\| "map_ltree h (QueryL y f) = QueryL (h y) (λd. map_ltree h (f d))"<br>\| "map_ltree h (QueryG y f) = QueryG y (λd. map_ltree h (f d))"<br>\| "map_ltree h (Side y d t) = Side y d (map_ltree h t)" | Context-sensitivity reindexes the local unknown `pp` to `pp × 'c`. A per-edge tree `apply_etf etf a u` queries only the single predecessor `u`, and a combine tree `etf_combine etf dst cc ex` queries the caller `cc` and the callee exit `ex`; so the context routing of either is captured by a position-aware relabelling `h :: pp ⇒ pp × 'c` of the `QueryL` targets (intra: `u ↦ (u, c)`; combine: caller `↦ (cc, c)`, callee `↦ (ex, c')`). `map_ltree` performs that relabelling; `traverse_rhs_map_ltree` shows it commutes with the denotation under the matching `map_sum`-pullback of the unknown environment, so a context-indexed equation system's denotation is the original one read against the relabelled environment. Globals (`QueryG` / `Side`) are untouched. |
| definition | `side_cfg_T_eff_ctx` | 427 | definition side_cfg_T_eff_ctx ::<br>  "('c ⇒ vname option ⇒ pp ⇒ pp<br>     ⇒ (pp × 'c, 'g, 'a abs_state) strategy_tree)<br>   ⇒ cfg ⇒ ('g, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) effectful_domain_transfer<br>   ⇒ 'a abs_state ⇒ 'a abs_state ⇒ 'g<br>   ⇒ (pp × 'c, 'g, 'a abs_state) eqsT"<br>where<br>  "side_cfg_T_eff_ctx cmb g etf bot0 s0 gseed =<br>     (λ(v, c).<br>        let acc0 = (if v = cfg_entry g then bot0 ⊔ restrict_local s0 else bot0);<br>            intra = map (λ(u, a). map_ltree (λw. (w, c)) (apply_etf etf a u))<br>                        (intra_predecessor_list g v);<br>            enter = map (λ(cl, fs, as). map_ltree (λw. (w, c)) (etf_enter etf fs as cl))<br>                        (entry_seed_list g v);<br>            comb  = map (λ(cc, dst, ex). cmb c dst cc ex) (return_call_list g v);<br>... (2 more lines) | The equation for `(v, c)` statically relabels ordinary-edge and entry trees to context `c`. Return/combine trees come from the supplied builder `cmb`, which may route the callee-exit query through a context computed from the caller state. Keeping `cmb` explicit separates context routing from the domain transfer record. |

### src/Analysis/Generic/Solver/Exec/Exec_Bridge.thy

> Generic (domain-agnostic) bridge between executable st side-effecting equation systems and abstract abs_state side_cfg_T_eff systems. Domain theories discharge per-tree traverse and side denotation commutation through fun_of_st.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type_synonym | `st_edge_tf_tree` | 61 | type_synonym ('g, 'c) st_edge_tf_tree =<br>  "pp ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'c) strategy_tree" | Executable counterpart of the effectful transfer record: per-action strategy-tree producers with payloads at `"'a st"` instead of `"'a abs_state"`. |
| type_synonym | `st_combine_tf_tree` | 64 | type_synonym ('g, 'c) st_combine_tf_tree =<br>  "pp ⇒ pp ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'c) strategy_tree" |  |
| record | `effectful_st_transfer` | 67 | record ('g, 'c) effectful_st_transfer =<br>  etf_st_nop        :: "('g, 'c) st_edge_tf_tree"<br>  etf_st_assign     :: "vname ⇒ aexp ⇒ ('g, 'c) st_edge_tf_tree"<br>  etf_st_assume     :: "bexp  ⇒ ('g, 'c) st_edge_tf_tree"<br>  etf_st_assume_not :: "bexp  ⇒ ('g, 'c) st_edge_tf_tree"<br>  etf_st_enter      :: "vname list ⇒ aexp list ⇒ ('g, 'c) st_edge_tf_tree"<br>  etf_st_combine    :: "vname option ⇒ ('g, 'c) st_combine_tf_tree" |  |
| fun | `apply_etf_st` | 75 | fun apply_etf_st ::<br>  "('g, 'c) effectful_st_transfer ⇒ edge_action ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'c) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "apply_etf_st etf EA_Nop           u = etf_st_nop etf u"<br>\| "apply_etf_st etf (EA_Assign x a)  u = etf_st_assign etf x a u"<br>\| "apply_etf_st etf (EA_Assume b)    u = etf_st_assume etf b u"<br>\| "apply_etf_st etf (EA_AssumeNot b) u = etf_st_assume_not etf b u"<br>\| "apply_etf_st etf (EA_Ret e p) u =<br>     (case e of None ⇒ etf_st_nop etf u \| Some a ⇒ etf_st_assign etf ret_var a u)" |  |
| fun | `etf_combine_st` | 86 | fun etf_combine_st ::<br>  "('g, 'c) effectful_st_transfer ⇒ vname option ⇒ pp ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'c) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "etf_combine_st etf dst cc ex = etf_st_combine etf dst cc ex" |  |
| definition | `unit_edge_tree_st` | 99 | definition unit_edge_tree_st ::<br>  "('a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot st ⇒ 'a st) ⇒ (unit, 'a st) st_edge_tf_tree"<br>where<br>  "unit_edge_tree_st f u =<br>     QueryL u (λsu. QueryG () (λg.<br>       let res = f (su ⊔ g) in<br>       Side () (restrict_global_st res)<br>         (Answer (restrict_local_st res))))" | The executable edge and combine trees preserve the unit-global routing shape while representing abstract states with `"'a st"`. |
| definition | `unit_combine_tree_st` | 108 | definition unit_combine_tree_st ::<br>  "vname option ⇒ pp ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp, unit, 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot st) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "unit_combine_tree_st dst cc ex =<br>     QueryL cc (λsc. QueryL ex (λse. QueryG () (λg.<br>       let res = combine_collect_abs_st dst (sc ⊔ g) (se ⊔ g) in<br>       Side () (restrict_global_st res)<br>         (Answer (restrict_local_st res)))))" |  |
| definition | `unit_etf_st_of_transfer` | 127 | definition unit_etf_st_of_transfer ::<br>  "(edge_action ⇒ 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot st ⇒ 'a st)<br>   ⇒ (vname list ⇒ aexp list ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'a st)<br>   ⇒ (unit, 'a st) effectful_st_transfer"<br>where<br>  "unit_etf_st_of_transfer tf_st enter_st = ⦇<br>    etf_st_nop        = unit_edge_tree_st (tf_st EA_Nop),<br>    etf_st_assign     = (λx e. unit_edge_tree_st (tf_st (EA_Assign x e))),<br>    etf_st_assume     = (λb. unit_edge_tree_st (tf_st (EA_Assume b))),<br>    etf_st_assume_not = (λb. unit_edge_tree_st (tf_st (EA_AssumeNot b))),<br>    etf_st_enter      = (λxs es. unit_edge_tree_st (enter_st xs es)),<br>    etf_st_combine    = unit_combine_tree_st<br>  ⦈" | Executable mirror of the abstract-side `unit_etf_of_transfer`: builds an `effectful_st_transfer` record from a single dispatch function and an enter function, both at `"'a st"`. Domain instances (`Sign_Exec`, `Ivl_Exec`) instantiate this once instead of hand-writing the six-field record. |
| primrec | `side_rg` | 214 | primrec side_rg ::<br>  "('x, 'g, ('a::bot) st) strategy_tree ⇒ bool"<br>where<br>  "side_rg (Answer d) = True"<br>\| "side_rg (QueryL y f) = (∀v. side_rg (f v))"<br>\| "side_rg (QueryG y f) = (∀v. side_rg (f v))"<br>\| "side_rg (Side y d t) = (restrict_global_st d = d ∧ side_rg t)" |  |
| locale | `sound_rhs_generator_exec` | 250 | locale sound_rhs_generator_exec = sound_rhs_generator_static +<br>  fixes F :: "edge_action ⇒ 'a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot abs_state ⇒ 'a abs_state"<br>    and etf_st :: "(unit, 'a st) effectful_st_transfer"<br>    and F_st :: "edge_action ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'a st"<br>  assumes edge: "⋀a u. apply_etf etf a u = unit_edge_tree (F a) u"<br>  assumes edge_st: "⋀a u. apply_etf_st etf_st a u = unit_edge_tree_st (F_st a) u"<br>  assumes comb_st: "⋀cc ex dst. etf_combine_st etf_st dst cc ex = unit_combine_tree_st dst cc ex"<br>  assumes commute: "⋀a s. fun_of_st (F_st a s) = F a (fun_of_st s)"<br>begin |  |
| definition | `side_contribution_trees_st` | 332 | definition side_contribution_trees_st ::<br>  "('g, ('a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) st) effectful_st_transfer<br>   ⇒ (pp × edge_action) list<br>   ⇒ (pp × vname list × aexp list) list<br>   ⇒ (pp × vname option × pp) list<br>   ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'a st) strategy_tree list"<br>where<br>  "side_contribution_trees_st etf es ens cs =<br>     map (λ(u, a). apply_etf_st etf a u) es @<br>     map (λ(cl, fs, as). etf_st_enter etf fs as cl) ens @<br>     map (λ(cc, dst, ex). etf_combine_st etf dst cc ex) cs" |  |

| definition | `make_side_rhs_tree_eff_st` | 389 | definition make_side_rhs_tree_eff_st ::<br>  "cfg ⇒ ('g, ('a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) st) effectful_st_transfer<br>   ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'g ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp, 'g, 'a st) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "make_side_rhs_tree_eff_st g etf bot0_st s0_st gseed v =<br>     (let acc0 = (if v = cfg_entry g then bot0_st ⊔ restrict_local_st s0_st else bot0_st);<br>          t    = side_rhs_fold_eff_st etf acc0<br>                   (intra_predecessor_list g v) (entry_seed_list g v) (return_call_list g v)<br>      in if v = cfg_entry g then Side gseed (restrict_global_st s0_st) t else t)" |  |

### src/Analysis/Generic/Solver/Exec/Solver_Menu.thy

> The vendored side solver comes in several *update rules* --- disciplines for how a global's incoming contributions are combined: plain join, per-origin join, Apinis warrowing (widening on globals), and their combinations. They share one signature, so an example can run the *same* equation system under each discipline and compare the results in a single `value` or lemma, instead of duplicating a `definition` plus a read per solver. • `join` --- `TD_side_always_join_Interp_solve`: fold every contribution into one slot, join. Precise; no termination guarantee for unbounded global chains. • `per_origin` --- `TD_side_per_origin_Interp_solve`: keep each write origin's contribution separate, join per origin. Reproduces digest separation without the solver knowing about digests. • `warrow` --- `TD_side_warrowing_apinis_Interp_solve`: widen globals for termination (narrow/warrow locals). Terminates on unbounded loops; over-approximates globals (collapses digest partitions). The menu omits `TD_side_warrowing_per_origin_Interp_solve` because that interpretation does not code-generate for these equation systems. It also omits `TD_side_bounded_narrowing_Interp_solve`, whose executable contract is not established here.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| type_synonym | `side_solver` | 50 | type_synonym ('x, 'g, 'd) side_solver =<br>  "('x, 'g, 'd) eqsT ⇒ 'x ⇒ ('x set × ('x + 'g ⇒ 'd))" |  |
| definition | `solver_menu` | 53 | definition solver_menu where<br>  "solver_menu =<br>     [(STR ''join'',       TD_side_always_join_Interp_solve),<br>      (STR ''per_origin'', TD_side_per_origin_Interp_solve),<br>      (STR ''warrow'',     TD_side_warrowing_apinis_Interp_solve)]" |  |
| definition | `run_menu` | 62 | definition run_menu where<br>  "run_menu eqs entry k var =<br>     map (λ(nm, solve). (nm, lookup_st (snd (solve eqs entry) k) var)) solver_menu"<br><br>end | Read one global/local slot's variable under every solver on the menu, in one call. `eqs` the equation system, `entry` the solver entry unknown, `k` the slot to read (e.g. `Inr ctx` for a keyed global), `var` the program variable. |

### src/Analysis/Generic/Solver/Exec/Solver_Side_RG.thy

> If every reachable side contribution is fixed by `restrict_global_st`, the solver keeps every global slot in that image: slots start at bottom, joins preserve the image, and only such contributions reach them. This yields `inr_slot_locals_bot` without a least-solution argument. The proof is domain-generic but intentionally phrased at the executable bridge's state projection.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| definition | `rg_val` | 13 | definition rg_val :: "('a::bot) st ⇒ bool" where<br>  "rg_val d ↔ restrict_global_st d = d" | If every reachable side contribution is fixed by `restrict_global_st`, the solver keeps every global slot in that image: slots start at bottom, joins preserve the image, and only such contributions reach them. This yields `inr_slot_locals_bot` without a least-solution argument. The proof is domain-generic but intentionally phrased at the executable bridge's state projection. |
| abbreviation | `rg_state` | 25 | abbreviation rg_state :: "(pp, unit, ('a::bot) st) state ⇒ bool" where<br>  "rg_state s ≡ (∀g. rg_val (state.σ s (Inr g)))" |  |
| abbreviation | `rg_sides` | 28 | abbreviation rg_sides :: "(unit ⇒ ('a::bot) st) ⇒ bool" where<br>  "rg_sides sa ≡ (∀g. rg_val (sa g))" |  |

| fun | `plus_ivl` | 8 | fun plus_ivl :: "ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; ivl" where<br>    "plus_ivl (Ivl l1 u1) (Ivl l2 u2) =<br>       (case (normalize_ivl (Ivl l1 u1), normalize_ivl (Ivl l2 u2)) of<br>          (Ivl a b, Ivl c d) ⇒ normalize_ivl (Ivl (a + c) (b + d)))"<br>instance ..<br>end<br><br>instantiation ivl :: minus begin |  |
| fun | `minus_ivl` | 16 | fun minus_ivl :: "ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; ivl" where<br>    "minus_ivl (Ivl l1 u1) (Ivl l2 u2) =<br>       (case (normalize_ivl (Ivl l1 u1), normalize_ivl (Ivl l2 u2)) of<br>          (Ivl a b, Ivl c d) ⇒ normalize_ivl (Ivl (a - d) (b - c)))"<br>instance ..<br>end |  |
| fun | `ivl_times_core` | 45 | fun ivl_times_core :: "ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; ivl" where<br>    "ivl_times_core (Ivl (Fin l1) (Fin u1)) (Ivl (Fin l2) (Fin u2)) =<br>       Ivl (Fin (min (l1*l2) (min (l1*u2) (min (u1*l2) (u1*u2)))))<br>           (Fin (max (l1*l2) (max (l1*u2) (max (u1*l2) (u1*u2)))))"<br>  \| "ivl_times_core _ _ = ivl_top"<br><br>instantiation ivl :: times begin | Precise multiplication on non-empty intervals. For all-finite operands the product range is the min / max over the four corner products (the box product attains its extrema at the corners); any infinite bound falls back to `ivl_top`. An empty operand yields `bot` -- the proper bottom handling that keeps the operator monotone (mapping empties to `ivl_top` would destroy the ordering). |
| definition | `times_ivl` | 52 | definition times_ivl :: "ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; ivl" where<br>  "times_ivl a b = (if ivl_nonempty a ∧ ivl_nonempty b then ivl_times_core a b else bot)"<br>instance ..<br>end |  |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Interval/Interval_Backward.thy

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| fun | `aval_ivl` | 9 | fun aval_ivl :: "aexp =&gt; (vname =&gt; ivl) =&gt; ivl" where<br>    "aval_ivl (N n)        σ = Ivl (Fin n) (Fin n)"<br>  \| "aval_ivl (V x)        σ = σ x"<br>  \| "aval_ivl (Plus  a b)  σ = aval_ivl a σ + aval_ivl b σ"<br>  \| "aval_ivl (Minus a b)  σ = aval_ivl a σ - aval_ivl b σ"<br>  \| "aval_ivl (Times a b)  σ = aval_ivl a σ * aval_ivl b σ" |  |
| fun | `inv_less_ivl` | 36 | fun inv_less_ivl :: "bool =&gt; ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; ivl * ivl" where<br>    "inv_less_ivl True  (Ivl l1 u1) (Ivl l2 u2) =<br>       (Ivl l1 u1 ⊓ Ivl MinInf (u2 - Fin 1),<br>        Ivl l2 u2 ⊓ Ivl (l1 + Fin 1) PlusInf)"<br>  \| "inv_less_ivl False (Ivl l1 u1) (Ivl l2 u2) =<br>       (Ivl l1 u1 ⊓ Ivl l2 PlusInf,<br>        Ivl l2 u2 ⊓ Ivl MinInf u1)" | Inverse operators for backward analysis over intervals. *inv_less_ivl* performs precise interval narrowing: on the true branch of *n1 < n2*, the upper bound of *n1* tightens to one below the upper bound of *n2*, and the lower bound of *n2* tightens to one above the lower bound of *n1*. On the false branch (*n1 ≥ n2*), *n1*`s` lower bound tightens to the lower bound of *n2*, and *n2*`s` upper bound tightens to the upper bound of *n1*. Plus/minus/times instantiate the shared `inv_conservative` (identity) instead of a per-domain no-op. |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Interval/Interval_DG.thy

> Interval uses the same carrier for local answers and global side effects. The diagonal specification `unit_dg_spec` therefore provides its D/G operations, while `sound_dg_spec` supplies collecting soundness.

| definition | `ivl_dg_gamma` | 20 | definition ivl_dg_gamma ::<br>  "(pp × unit + unit ⇒ (ivl abs_state, ivl abs_state) dg_state)<br>   ⇒ pp ⇒ store set"<br>where<br>  "ivl_dg_gamma ≡ sound_dg_spec.dg_gamma gamma_unit" |  |
| definition | `ivl_dg_generator` | 26 | definition ivl_dg_generator ::<br>  "cfg ⇒ ivl abs_state ⇒ ivl abs_state ⇒ ivl abs_state<br>   ⇒ (pp × unit, unit, (ivl abs_state, ivl abs_state) dg_state) eqsT"<br>where<br>  "ivl_dg_generator ≡ sound_dg_spec.dg_gen (unit_dg_spec ivl_tf)" |  |
| locale | `ivl_dg_api` | 41 | locale ivl_dg_api<br><br>sublocale ivl_dg_api ⊆ sound_dg_spec "unit_dg_spec ivl_tf" gamma_unit<br>  rewrites "sound_dg_spec.dg_gamma gamma_unit = ivl_dg_gamma"<br>       and "sound_dg_spec.dg_gen (unit_dg_spec ivl_tf) = ivl_dg_generator"<br>  apply (rule sound_dg_spec_unit[OF ivl_is_sound_transfer])<br>   apply (simp add: ivl_dg_gamma_def)<br>  apply (simp add: ivl_dg_generator_def)<br>  done<br><br>context ivl_dg_api<br>begin | Public DG API for the Interval domain. The sublocale interpretation rewrites facts inherited from `sound_dg_spec` into this native vocabulary, so the exported theorem below cites the generic collecting-soundness fact directly with no manual unfold/fold bridging. The trailing bare interpretation flattens the locale so consumers see the same top-level names as before; `ivl_dg` above stays untouched for the existing `ivl_dg.`-qualified call sites. |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Interval/Interval_Lattice.thy

> An interval *[l, u]* abstracts the integers *{n. l ≤ n ∧ n ≤ u*"}. Bounds are extended integers (*MinInf* / *Fin n* / *PlusInf*). Empty (e.g. *[+inf, -inf]*) is *bot*; *[-inf, +inf]* is *top*.

| definition | `less_eq_ivl` | 23 | definition less_eq_ivl :: "ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; bool" where<br>  "(a::ivl) &lt;= b = (case (a, b) of (Ivl l1 u1, Ivl l2 u2) =&gt; l2 ≤ l1 ∧ u1 ≤ u2)" |  |
| definition | `less_ivl` | 25 | definition less_ivl :: "ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; bool" where<br>  "(a::ivl) &lt; b = (a &lt;= b ∧ ¬ b &lt;= a)"<br>instance ..<br>end<br><br>instantiation ivl :: bot begin |  |
| definition | `bot_ivl` | 31 | definition bot_ivl :: ivl where<br>  "bot_ivl = Ivl PlusInf MinInf"<br>instance ..<br>end<br><br>instantiation ivl :: order begin<br>instance proof intro_classes<br>  fix x y z :: ivl<br>  show "(x &lt; y) = (x ≤ y ∧ ¬ y ≤ x)"<br>    unfolding less_ivl_def by simp<br>  show "x ≤ x"<br>    unfolding less_eq_ivl_def by (cases x) (simp add: eint_le_refl)<br>  show "x ≤ y ⟹ y ≤ z ⟹ x ≤ z"<br>    unfolding less_eq_ivl_def by (cases x; cases y; cases z) (auto intro: eint_le_trans)<br>  show "x ≤ y ⟹ y ≤ x ⟹ x = y"<br>... (11 more lines) |  |
| definition | `ivl_top` | 58 | definition ivl_top :: ivl where<br>  "ivl_top = Ivl MinInf PlusInf" |  |
| fun | `gamma_ivl` | 63 | fun gamma_ivl :: "ivl =&gt; int set" where<br>    "gamma_ivl (Ivl l u) = {n. l ≤ Fin n ∧ Fin n ≤ u}" |  |
| fun | `join_ivl` | 86 | fun join_ivl :: "ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; ivl" where<br>    "join_ivl (Ivl l1 u1) (Ivl l2 u2) =<br>       Ivl (if l1 ≤ l2 then l1 else l2)<br>           (if u2 ≤ u1 then u1 else u2)" |  |

| fun | `meet_ivl` | 144 | fun meet_ivl :: "ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; ivl" where<br>    "meet_ivl (Ivl l1 u1) (Ivl l2 u2) =<br>       Ivl (if l2 ≤ l1 then l1 else l2)<br>           (if u1 ≤ u2 then u1 else u2)"<br><br>instantiation ivl :: inf begin | Interval intersection. Used by *assume_ivl* to refine a variable's interval on a guard (*x < n* narrows the upper bound). |
| definition | `inf_ivl` | 150 | definition inf_ivl :: "ivl ⇒ ivl ⇒ ivl" where "inf_ivl = meet_ivl"<br>instance ..<br>end<br>declare inf_ivl_def [simp] |  |
| definition | `gamma_abs_ivl` | 204 | definition gamma_abs_ivl [simp]: "gamma (a :: ivl) = gamma_ivl a"<br>instance proof intro_classes<br>  show "gamma (bot :: ivl) = {}"<br>    by (simp add: gamma_ivl_bot)<br>next<br>  fix a b :: ivl<br>  assume H: "a ≤ b"<br>  show "gamma a ⊆ gamma b"<br>  proof -<br>    have "gamma_ivl a ⊆ gamma_ivl b" using H by (rule gamma_ivl_mono)<br>    then show ?thesis by simp<br>  qed<br>qed<br>end |  |
| definition | `normalize_ivl` | 233 | definition normalize_ivl :: "ivl ⇒ ivl" where<br>  "normalize_ivl v =<br>     (case v of Ivl l u ⇒<br>        if l ≤ u ∧ l ≠ PlusInf ∧ u ≠ MinInf then v else bot)" | **Canonical empty interval.** The raw `ivl` lattice has infinitely many empty representations (any `Ivl l u` with `l > u`, or an infinite bound on the wrong side). `normalize_ivl` collapses every empty interval to the single `bot` representative `Ivl PlusInf MinInf`, leaving non-empty intervals untouched. Arithmetic feeds its operands through `normalize_ivl` first. Since the pointwise sum/difference of `bot` with anything is again `bot`, this keeps every empty result canonical. Without it, `[1,0] + [1,1] = [2,1]` would manufacture ever-new empty representations, which as context keys defeat fixpoint convergence in the recursive interval example. |
| fun | `ivl_nonempty` | 257 | fun ivl_nonempty :: "ivl =&gt; bool" where<br>  "ivl_nonempty (Ivl l u) = (l ≤ u ∧ l ≠ PlusInf ∧ u ≠ MinInf)" | Emptiness test on intervals. The raw `ivl` lattice contains many unnormalised empty intervals (any ``Ivl l u`` with *l > u*, including `bot`). *ivl_nonempty* captures exactly the intervals with non-empty concretization (proved below: implied by any witness, and monotone along *≤*); multiplication uses it for proper bottom handling, which is what makes the precise corner product monotone. |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Interval/Interval_Point_Digest.thy

The exactness obligation holds because `gamma_ivl (Ivl (Fin v) (Fin v)) = {v}`. |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Interval/Interval_Transfer.thy

> Both guards delegate to the generic *bfilter* proved sound in `backward_domain`. *assume_ivl b σ* filters for *bval b*, *assume_not_ivl b σ* for *¬ bval b*.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |

| definition | `assume_not_ivl` | 23 | definition assume_not_ivl :: "bexp =&gt; (vname =&gt; ivl) =&gt; (vname =&gt; ivl)" where<br>  "assume_not_ivl b σ = bfilter_ivl b False σ" |  |
| definition | `assign_ivl` | 31 | definition assign_ivl ::<br>    "vname =&gt; aexp =&gt; (vname =&gt; ivl) =&gt; (vname =&gt; ivl)"<br>where<br>  "assign_ivl x a σ = σ(x := aval_ivl a σ)" |  |
| definition | `enter_frame_ivl` | 48 | definition enter_frame_ivl :: "ivl abs_state ⇒ ivl abs_state" where<br>  "enter_frame_ivl = enter_frame_D ivl_top" | Procedure entry: keep globals, reset locals to the full interval, then bind the formals to the abstract values of the actuals evaluated in the caller. Generic via enter_frame_D/enter_D (Constraint_System.thy), parameterised by ivl_top as the domain's fully-imprecise reset value. |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Interval/Interval_Warrowing.thy

> Standard interval widening: keep a bound if it did not move outward, otherwise push it to the infinite end. Every widen-ascending chain therefore stabilises after at most two steps (each bound takes at most one outward jump).

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| fun | `widen_ivl_core` | 15 | fun widen_ivl_core :: "ivl =&gt; ivl =&gt; ivl" where<br>    "widen_ivl_core (Ivl l1 u1) (Ivl l2 u2) =<br>       Ivl (if l1 ≤ l2 then l1 else MinInf)<br>           (if u2 ≤ u1 then u1 else PlusInf)" | Standard interval widening: keep a bound if it did not move outward, otherwise push it to the infinite end. Every widen-ascending chain therefore stabilises after at most two steps (each bound takes at most one outward jump). |
| fun | `narrow_ivl_td` | 168 | fun narrow_ivl_td :: "ivl ⇒ ivl ⇒ ivl" where<br>  "narrow_ivl_td (Ivl l1 u1) (Ivl l2 u2) =<br>     Ivl (if l1 = MinInf then l2 else l1) (if u1 = PlusInf then u2 else u1)"<br><br>instantiation ivl :: warrowing begin | Standard interval narrowing: when the recomputed value `b` refines the widened value `a` (`b ≤ a`), keep `a`'s finite bounds and only fill an infinite bound of `a` from `b`. A bound moves off `±inf` at most once, so the narrowing descent is finite. Combined with the backward guard filters this recovers loop bounds under *every* update rule (a bounded local counter reads `[0, 20]` under `join`, `per_origin` and `warrow` alike). It does not help a *flow-insensitive global*: there the guard never bounds the value written back to the slot, so `[0, +inf]` is a genuine fixpoint and narrowing has nothing smaller to descend to. |
| definition | `widen` | 177 | definition "widen (a :: ivl) b =<br>     (if a = bot then b else if b = bot then a else widen_ivl_core a b)" | Widening carries the standard bot-law `bot ∇ x = x`, `x ∇ bot = x`: since `bot :: ivl` is the empty interval `Ivl PlusInf MinInf`, an unguarded `widen_ivl_core` from bot would jump straight to the top interval, topping any unknown on its first stabilisation. The guard keeps the first contribution exact. |
| definition | `narrow` | 179 | definition "narrow (a :: ivl) b = narrow_ivl_td a b"<br>instance proof intro_classes<br>  fix a b :: ivl<br>  show "a ≤ widen a b"<br>  proof (cases "a = bot")<br>    case True thus ?thesis by (simp add: widen_ivl_def)<br>  next<br>    case False thus ?thesis<br>      by (cases "b = bot") (simp_all add: widen_ivl_def a_le_widen_ivl_core bot.extremum)<br>  qed<br>  show "b ≤ widen a b"<br>  proof (cases "a = bot")<br>    case True thus ?thesis by (simp add: widen_ivl_def)<br>  next<br>    case False thus ?thesis<br>... (12 more lines) |  |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Mixed/Exec_DG_Bridge.thy

> The verified solver uses the executable association-list carrier `'a st`, while soundness is stated over function-valued abstract states. `fun_of_dg_st` lifts the refinement morphism `fun_of_st` to the D/G product and commutes with equation evaluation. D/G lattice operations are componentwise, so the product inherits the order, join, bottom, equality, and widening operations required by the solver.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| definition | `fun_of_dg_st` | 31 | definition fun_of_dg_st ::<br>  "(('a::bot) st, ('b::bot) st) dg_state ⇒ ('a abs_state, 'b abs_state) dg_state"<br>where<br>  "fun_of_dg_st d = DG (fun_of_st (locals d)) (fun_of_st (globs d))" |  |
| definition | `unit_step_st` | 66 | definition unit_step_st ::<br>  "(('a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) st ⇒ 'a st) ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'a st × 'a st"<br>where<br>  "unit_step_st f d g = (let res = f (d ⊔ g) in (restrict_global_st res, restrict_local_st res))" | Executable diagonal step and combine operations act on `'a st`. Their proofs are domain-independent: any executable transfer that commutes through `fun_of_st` yields a commuting D/G step. |
| definition | `unit_combine_step_st_env` | 73 | definition unit_combine_step_st_env ::<br>  "('a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) st ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'a st × 'a st"<br>where<br>  "unit_combine_step_st_env dc de g =<br>     (let m = combine_abs_st (dc ⊔ g) (de ⊔ g)<br>      in (restrict_global_st m, restrict_local_st m))" | Executable mirror of the abstract-side `unit_combine_step_env`/ `unit_combine_step_assign` split. |
| definition | `unit_combine_step_st_assign` | 80 | definition unit_combine_step_st_assign ::<br>  "vname option ⇒ ('a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) st ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'a st × 'a st<br>   ⇒ 'a st × 'a st"<br>where<br>  "unit_combine_step_st_assign dst de g merged =<br>     (let res = combine_assign_abs_st dst (lookup_st (de ⊔ g) ret_var) (fst merged ⊔ snd merged)<br>      in (restrict_global_st res, restrict_local_st res))" |  |
| definition | `unit_dg_spec_st` | 95 | definition unit_dg_spec_st ::<br>  "(edge_action ⇒ ('a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) st ⇒ 'a st)<br>   ⇒ (vname list ⇒ aexp list ⇒ 'a st ⇒ 'a st)<br>   ⇒ ('a st, 'a st) dg_spec"<br>where<br>  "unit_dg_spec_st tf_st enter_st = ⦇<br>    dgs_nop        = unit_step_st (tf_st EA_Nop),<br>    dgs_assign     = (λx e. unit_step_st (tf_st (EA_Assign x e))),<br>    dgs_assume     = (λb. unit_step_st (tf_st (EA_Assume b))),<br>    dgs_assume_not = (λb. unit_step_st (tf_st (EA_AssumeNot b))),<br>    dgs_enter      = (λxs es. unit_step_st (enter_st xs es)),<br>    dgs_combine_env    = unit_combine_step_st_env,<br>    dgs_combine_assign = unit_combine_step_st_assign<br>  ⦈" |  |
| definition | `dg_cmb_of` | 239 | definition dg_cmb_of ::<br>  "(('d::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot), ('h::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot)) dg_spec<br>     ⇒ (pp ⇒ unit ⇒ 'd ⇒ call_action ⇒ unit) ⇒ unit ⇒ call_action ⇒ pp ⇒ pp<br>     ⇒ (pp × unit, unit, ('d, 'h) dg_state) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "dg_cmb_of S route ctx ca cc ex =<br>     (case ca of CallEdge dst _ _ ⇒<br>       map_gtree (λ_. ()) (map_ltree (λw. (w, ctx)) (dg_spec_combine_tree S dst cc ex)))" | The executable generator is the same polymorphic seeded keyed generator (`side_cfg_T_eff_keyed_seed_dg`) the abstract `sound_dg_spec.dg_gen` uses, instantiated at an `'a st`-valued analysis spec. Unit context (`gkey = (λ_. ())`), no procedure-entry seed (`frame_seed = (λ_. bot)`). |
| definition | `dg_extra_of` | 248 | definition dg_extra_of ::<br>  "(('d::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot), ('h::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot)) dg_spec ⇒ cfg<br>     ⇒ (pp ⇒ unit ⇒ 'd ⇒ call_action ⇒ unit) ⇒ unit ⇒ pp<br>     ⇒ (pp × unit, unit, ('d, 'h) dg_state) strategy_tree list"<br>where<br>  "dg_extra_of S g route ctx v =<br>     map (λ(cl, ca). case ca of CallEdge dst fs as ⇒<br>       map_gtree (λ_. ()) (map_ltree (λw. (w, ctx))<br>         (dg_edge_tree (dgs_enter S fs as) cl))) (entry_call_list g v)" |  |
| definition | `dg_gen_of` | 258 | definition dg_gen_of ::<br>  "(('d::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot), ('h::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot)) dg_spec ⇒ cfg ⇒ 'd ⇒ 'd ⇒ 'h<br>     ⇒ (pp × unit, unit, ('d, 'h) dg_state) eqsT"<br>where<br>  "dg_gen_of S g bot0 s0d s0g =<br>     side_cfg_T_eff_keyed_seed_dg intra_predecessor_list (λ_. ())<br>       (λ_ _ _ _. ()) (dg_cmb_of S) (dg_extra_of S g) g S bot0 s0d s0g" |  |
| definition | `dg_tree_st_commute` | 453 | definition dg_tree_st_commute ::<br>  "('u + 'k ⇒ (('a::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) st, ('b::bounded_semilattice_sup_bot) st) dg_state)<br>   ⇒ ('u, 'k, ('a st, 'b st) dg_state) strategy_tree<br>   ⇒ ('u, 'k, ('a abs_state, 'b abs_state) dg_state) strategy_tree ⇒ bool"<br>where<br>  "dg_tree_st_commute σ_st t_st t_abs ↔<br>     fun_of_dg_st (traverse_rhs t_st σ_st) = traverse_rhs t_abs (fun_of_dg_st ∘ σ_st)<br>   ∧ (∀k. fun_of_dg_st (sides_of_rhs t_st σ_st k) = sides_of_rhs t_abs (fun_of_dg_st ∘ σ_st) k)<br>   ∧ dep_aux σ_st t_st = dep_aux (fun_of_dg_st ∘ σ_st) t_abs" | `dg_tree_st_commute σ_st t_st t_abs` is the reusable transport contract for a single strategy tree: its executable denotation, its side-effect map, and its static dependencies all agree (through `fun_of_dg_st`) with the abstract tree read against the pushed-forward valuation `fun_of_dg_st ∘ σ_st`. It bundles the three commutation obligations the equation-system transport threads through the accumulator fold. The intra per-edge trees are discharged generically below from a componentwise analysis step. The opaque `cmb`/`extra` trees --- whose `Side` targets may be computed inside a `QueryL`/`QueryG` continuation (a routed callee seed) --- are supplied by the instance as bundled hypotheses; the bridge never assumes their side targets are syntactically fixed. The context is bound as `ctx`, never `c`: an unqualified `c` is captured by the imported constant `state.c`, which silently pins these theorems to a single context and produces a proof that only looks polymorphic. |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Mixed/Mixed_Sign_Interval.thy

> The answer domain D is a flow-sensitive Sign store: every CFG point has its own Sign answer. The side domain G is one flow-insensitive Interval invariant shared by every equation. The name `globs` identifies the solver's side slot; it does not restrict G to VIMP variables satisfying `is_global`. The analysis chooses which facts G contains. Every edge advances the two abstractions independently. Its Sign result becomes the successor's answer, while its Interval result is published to the shared side unknown. Solver joins therefore close G under every reachable transfer.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| definition | `mixed_si_spec` | 30 | definition mixed_si_spec :: "(sign abs_state, ivl abs_state) dg_spec" where<br>  "mixed_si_spec = indep_dg_spec sign_tf ivl_tf" | Sign advances the local slot, Interval the global slot, independently --- exactly `indep_dg_spec` instantiated at the two domain transfers, so no per-analysis re-derivation of the step/enter/combine fields is needed. |
| definition | `mixed_si_cmb` | 42 | definition mixed_si_cmb ::<br>  "(pp ⇒ unit ⇒ sign abs_state ⇒ call_action ⇒ unit) ⇒ unit ⇒ call_action ⇒ pp ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp × unit, unit,<br>       (sign abs_state, ivl abs_state) dg_state) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "mixed_si_cmb = dg_cmb_of mixed_si_spec" | Mixed's combine, entry-seed, and equation-generator trees are the generic D/G executable helpers (`dg_cmb_of`, `dg_extra_of`, `dg_gen_of` in *Exec_DG_Bridge*) instantiated at `mixed_si_spec`; no per-domain reimplementation is needed. |
| definition | `mixed_si_extra` | 49 | definition mixed_si_extra ::<br>  "cfg ⇒ (pp ⇒ unit ⇒ sign abs_state ⇒ call_action ⇒ unit) ⇒ unit ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp × unit, unit,<br>       (sign abs_state, ivl abs_state) dg_state) strategy_tree list"<br>where<br>  "mixed_si_extra = dg_extra_of mixed_si_spec" |  |
| definition | `mixed_si_generator` | 56 | definition mixed_si_generator ::<br>  "cfg ⇒ sign abs_state ⇒ sign abs_state ⇒ ivl abs_state<br>   ⇒ (pp × unit, unit,<br>       (sign abs_state, ivl abs_state) dg_state) eqsT"<br>where<br>  "mixed_si_generator = dg_gen_of mixed_si_spec" |  |
| definition | `mixed_si_D` | 63 | definition mixed_si_D ::<br>  "(pp × unit + unit ⇒<br>      (sign abs_state, ivl abs_state) dg_state)<br>   ⇒ pp ⇒ sign abs_state"<br>where<br>  "mixed_si_D sigma v = locals (sigma (Inl (v, ())))" |  |
| definition | `mixed_si_G` | 70 | definition mixed_si_G ::<br>  "(pp × unit + unit ⇒<br>      (sign abs_state, ivl abs_state) dg_state)<br>   ⇒ ivl abs_state"<br>where<br>  "mixed_si_G sigma = globs (sigma (Inr ()))" |  |

| definition | `mixed_si_postfix` | 99 | definition mixed_si_postfix ::<br>  "cfg ⇒ sign abs_state ⇒ ivl abs_state<br>   ⇒ (pp × unit + unit ⇒<br>        (sign abs_state, ivl abs_state) dg_state)<br>   ⇒ bool"<br>where<br>  "mixed_si_postfix g s0d s0g sigma =<br>     mixed_si.dg_postfix g s0d s0g sigma" |  |

(27 more lines) | Public DG API for the Sign/Interval mixed analysis, mirroring `Sign_DG.thy`/`Interval_DG.thy`'s pattern. The sublocale interpretation rewrites facts inherited from `sound_dg_spec` into this native vocabulary in one step, replacing the per-constant bridging lemmas above with a single rewrite obligation each; the exported theorems below then cite the generic facts directly, with no manual unfold/fold. |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Mixed/Rel_Order_Domain.thy

> `relc` tracks a finite set of known pairwise-ordered variables, `(x, y)` meaning `x ≤ y` at every store the value describes. No closure: two known facts `x ≤ y` and `y ≤ z` do not automatically yield `x ≤ z` in this carrier. This is deliberately the least amount of relational structure that is still relational (a pair of variables, not one) and not `abs_state` (no `vname ⇒ 'a` function type anywhere in the carrier). The purpose of this file is not a useful analysis. It demonstrates that a non-`abs_state` carrier discharges `sound_dg_spec` with zero changes to the DG framework (`../../../../docs/RELATIONAL_DOMAIN_ARCHITECTURE_DECISION.md`). Every transfer below is deliberately the most imprecise sound choice (forget on assign, havoc on call) except for a precise `assume`/ `assume_not` pair, which is enough to make the carrier genuinely relational.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| datatype | `relc` | 27 | datatype relc = Bot \| RelC "(vname × vname) set" |  |
| fun | `less_eq_relc` | 48 | fun less_eq_relc :: "relc ⇒ relc ⇒ bool" where<br>  "less_eq_relc Bot _ = True"<br>\| "less_eq_relc (RelC _) Bot = False"<br>\| "less_eq_relc (RelC a) (RelC b) = (b ⊆ a)" |  |
| definition | `less_relc` | 53 | definition less_relc :: "relc ⇒ relc ⇒ bool" where<br>  "less_relc a b ↔ a ≤ b ∧ ¬ b ≤ a" |  |
| fun | `sup_relc` | 56 | fun sup_relc :: "relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc" where<br>  "sup_relc Bot b = b"<br>\| "sup_relc a Bot = a"<br>\| "sup_relc (RelC a) (RelC b) = RelC (a ∩ b)" |  |
| definition | `bot_relc` | 61 | definition bot_relc :: relc where<br>  "bot_relc = Bot"<br><br>instance<br>proof intro_classes<br>  fix x y z :: relc<br>  show "x &lt; y ↔ x ≤ y ∧ ¬ y ≤ x" by (simp add: less_relc_def)<br>  show "x ≤ x" by (cases x) simp_all<br>  show "x ≤ y ⟹ y ≤ z ⟹ x ≤ z" by (cases x; cases y; cases z) auto<br>  show "x ≤ y ⟹ y ≤ x ⟹ x = y" by (cases x; cases y) auto<br>  show "x ≤ x ⊔ y" by (cases x; cases y) auto<br>  show "y ≤ x ⊔ y" by (cases x; cases y) auto<br>  show "y ≤ x ⟹ z ≤ x ⟹ y ⊔ z ≤ x" by (cases x; cases y; cases z) auto<br>  show "bot ≤ x" by (cases x) (simp_all add: bot_relc_def)<br>qed<br>... (2 more lines) |  |
| definition | `widen_relc` | 91 | definition widen_relc :: "relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc" where<br>  "widen_relc a b = a ⊔ b" |  |
| definition | `narrow_relc` | 94 | definition narrow_relc :: "relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc" where<br>  "narrow_relc a b = b"<br><br>instance<br>proof intro_classes<br>  fix a b :: relc<br>  show "a ≤ a ∇ b" by (simp add: widen_relc_def)<br>  show "b ≤ a ∇ b" by (simp add: widen_relc_def)<br>  show "b ≤ a ⟹ b ≤ a \&lt;Delta&gt; b" by (simp add: narrow_relc_def)<br>  show "b ≤ a ⟹ a \&lt;Delta&gt; b ≤ a" by (simp add: narrow_relc_def)<br>qed<br><br>end |  |
| definition | `top_relc` | 119 | definition top_relc :: relc where<br>  "top_relc = RelC {}" | `top_relc` is the empty-relation-set top element: vacuously true of every pair, so its concretization is `UNIV` (`gamma_rel_top`). |
| fun | `gamma_rel` | 124 | fun gamma_rel :: "relc ⇒ store set" where<br>  "gamma_rel Bot = {}"<br>\| "gamma_rel (RelC ps) = {s. ∀(x, y) ∈ ps. s x ≤ s y}" |  |
| fun | `relc_has` | 132 | fun relc_has :: "vname ⇒ vname ⇒ relc ⇒ bool" where<br>  "relc_has x y Bot = True"<br>\| "relc_has x y (RelC ps) = ((x, y) ∈ ps)" | Executable membership reader, the `relc` analogue of `lookup_st` for downstream examples. `Bot` answers `True` for every pair: its concretization is empty, so every fact holds of it vacuously -- consistent with `gamma_rel`, and it never needs to materialize `UNIV`. |
| fun | `string_of_pairs` | 143 | fun string_of_pairs :: "(vname × vname) list ⇒ string" where<br>  "string_of_pairs [] = ''''"<br>\| "string_of_pairs [(x, y)] = x @ ''&lt;='' @ y"<br>\| "string_of_pairs ((x, y) # p # ps) = x @ ''&lt;='' @ y @ '', '' @ string_of_pairs (p # ps)" | Pretty-printer, the `relc` analogue of Interval's `string_of_ivl` for GraphViz/console display. `vname × vname` is `linorder` (via `HOL-Library.Product_Lexorder`, already imported transitively by every file in this session that touches `cfg`), so `sorted_list_of_set` gives a deterministic, executable enumeration -- the same device this project already relies on for CFG edge sets. |
| definition | `string_of_relc` | 148 | definition string_of_relc :: "relc ⇒ string" where<br>  "string_of_relc d =<br>     (case d of<br>        Bot ⇒ ''BOT''<br>      \| RelC ps ⇒<br>          (if ps = {} then ''(no known relations)''<br>           else string_of_pairs (sorted_list_of_set ps)))" |  |
| definition | `gammaDG_rel` | 156 | definition gammaDG_rel :: "relc ⇒ relc ⇒ store set" where<br>  "gammaDG_rel d g = gamma_rel d ∩ gamma_rel g" |  |
| fun | `forget_relc` | 178 | fun forget_relc :: "vname ⇒ relc ⇒ relc" where<br>  "forget_relc x Bot = Bot"<br>\| "forget_relc x (RelC ps) = RelC {(a, b) ∈ ps. a ≠ x ∧ b ≠ x}" |  |
| definition | `assume_step` | 189 | definition assume_step :: "bexp ⇒ relc ⇒ relc" where<br>  "assume_step b d =<br>     (case d of<br>        Bot ⇒ Bot<br>      \| RelC ps ⇒<br>          (case b of<br>             Less (V x) (V y) ⇒ RelC (insert (x, y) ps)<br>           \| _ ⇒ RelC ps))" |  |
| definition | `assume_not_step` | 241 | definition assume_not_step :: "bexp ⇒ relc ⇒ relc" where<br>  "assume_not_step b d =<br>     (case d of<br>        Bot ⇒ Bot<br>      \| RelC ps ⇒<br>          (case b of<br>             Less (V x) (V y) ⇒ RelC (insert (y, x) ps)<br>           \| _ ⇒ RelC ps))" | The negated-guard counterpart: `¬(x < y)` is `y ≤ x`, the mirror image of `assume_step`'s one precise case, recorded on the false branch instead of discarded. Every other shape falls back exactly as `assume_step` does. |
| definition | `dgs_nop_rel` | 294 | definition dgs_nop_rel :: "relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc × relc" where<br>  "dgs_nop_rel d g = (g, d)" | Every step except the two precise `assume`/`assume_not` cases above is sound by forgetting or by leaving the carrier untouched -- deliberately imprecise: no closure, no normalization, havoc-based calls. |
| definition | `dgs_assign_rel` | 297 | definition dgs_assign_rel :: "vname ⇒ aexp ⇒ relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc × relc" where<br>  "dgs_assign_rel x e d g = (forget_relc x g, forget_relc x d)" |  |
| definition | `dgs_assume_rel` | 300 | definition dgs_assume_rel :: "bexp ⇒ relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc × relc" where<br>  "dgs_assume_rel b d g = (g, assume_step b d)" |  |
| definition | `dgs_assume_not_rel` | 303 | definition dgs_assume_not_rel :: "bexp ⇒ relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc × relc" where<br>  "dgs_assume_not_rel b d g = (g, assume_not_step b d)" |  |
| definition | `dgs_enter_rel` | 306 | definition dgs_enter_rel :: "vname list ⇒ aexp list ⇒ relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc × relc" where<br>  "dgs_enter_rel xs es dc g = (top_relc, top_relc)" |  |
| definition | `dgs_combine_env_rel` | 309 | definition dgs_combine_env_rel :: "relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc × relc" where<br>  "dgs_combine_env_rel dc de g = (top_relc, top_relc)" |  |
| definition | `dgs_combine_assign_rel` | 312 | definition dgs_combine_assign_rel ::<br>  "vname option ⇒ relc ⇒ relc ⇒ relc × relc ⇒ relc × relc"<br>where<br>  "dgs_combine_assign_rel dst de g merged = merged" |  |
| definition | `rel_order_spec` | 317 | definition rel_order_spec :: "(relc, relc) dg_spec" where<br>  "rel_order_spec = ⦇<br>     dgs_nop = dgs_nop_rel,<br>     dgs_assign = dgs_assign_rel,<br>     dgs_assume = dgs_assume_rel,<br>     dgs_assume_not = dgs_assume_not_rel,<br>     dgs_enter = dgs_enter_rel,<br>     dgs_combine_env = dgs_combine_env_rel,<br>     dgs_combine_assign = dgs_combine_assign_rel<br>   ⦈" |  |

### src/Analysis/Instances/NamedGlobalSign/Sign_Named_Global_Eff.thy

> The effectful transfer routes contributions to two distinct global unknowns. Its assignment tree reads the abstract value of *''Gflag''* and selects a target from its sign. Reassembling the state joins every side contribution, so routing preserves soundness while keeping unrelated fixed-point slots separate.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| datatype | `gname` | 19 | datatype gname = Gpos \| Gneg |  |
| definition | `route_tree` | 65 | definition route_tree ::<br>  "(sign abs_state ⇒ gname) ⇒ (sign abs_state ⇒ sign abs_state) ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp, gname, sign abs_state) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "route_tree route f u =<br>     read_local_cont u (λsu. read_global_cont Gpos (λgp. read_global_cont Gneg (λgn.<br>       let env = su ⊔ gp ⊔ gn; res = f env<br>       in depend_on (route env) (restrict_global res) (answer (restrict_local res)))))" | route_tree queries the local unknown at u and both global slots, reassembles the source state, applies a pure abstract step f, and emits the global part to a routed slot. Because etf_full joins all Side contributions, etf_full collapses to f on the source state regardless of the routing (route_tree_etf_full). |
| definition | `route_combine` | 81 | definition route_combine ::<br>  "(sign abs_state ⇒ gname) ⇒ vname option ⇒ pp ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp, gname, sign abs_state) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "route_combine route dst cc ex =<br>     read_local_cont cc (λsc. read_local_cont ex (λse. read_global_cont Gpos (λgp. read_global_cont Gneg (λgn.<br>       let envc = sc ⊔ gp ⊔ gn; enve = se ⊔ gp ⊔ gn;<br>           res = combine_collect_abs dst envc enve<br>       in depend_on (route envc) (restrict_global res) (answer (restrict_local res))))))" |  |
| definition | `named_etf` | 242 | definition named_etf :: "(gname, sign) effectful_domain_transfer" where<br>  "named_etf =<br>     ⦇ etf_nop        = route_tree (λ_. Gpos) (apply_tf sign_tf EA_Nop),<br>       etf_assign     = λx a. route_tree (λ_. Gpos) (apply_tf sign_tf (EA_Assign x a)),<br>       etf_assume     = λb. route_tree (λ_. Gpos) (apply_tf sign_tf (EA_Assume b)),<br>       etf_assume_not = λb. route_tree (λ_. Gpos) (apply_tf sign_tf (EA_AssumeNot b)),<br>       etf_enter      = (λxs es. route_tree (λ_. Gpos) (tf_enter sign_tf xs es)),<br>       etf_combine    = route_combine (λ_. Gneg) ⦈" | To carry a genuinely named-global analysis through the real solver, route with CONSTANT slots: edge contributions to Gpos, combine (procedure-return) contributions to Gneg. Both named slots are populated and the routing is monotone (unlike a routing that depends on the reassembled state, which is not σ-monotone and fails the TD_side mono_sides precondition), so the three TD_side preconditions discharge from the generic per-tree lemmas in TD_Side_Eff_Bounds. |
| definition | `sideg_tree` | 464 | definition sideg_tree ::<br>  "gname ⇒ gname ⇒ (sign abs_state ⇒ sign abs_state) ⇒ pp<br>   ⇒ (pp, gname, sign abs_state) strategy_tree"<br>where<br>  "sideg_tree gread gwrite f u =<br>     QueryL u (λsu. QueryG gread (λg.<br>       let res = f (su ⊔ g)<br>       in Side gwrite (restrict_global res) (Answer (restrict_local res))))" | `sideg_tree` queries the local unknown and exactly one named global, then routes the global result to one selected slot. Its soundness statement therefore uses `side_env_g` rather than the join of every global slot. |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Parity/Parity_Domain.thy

> parity abstracts integers by their parity: Bot -- empty (unreachable) Even -- {n \| even n} Odd -- {n \| odd n} Top -- all integers Four-element lattice (Bot \<sqsubseteq> Even,Odd \<sqsubseteq> Top). Finite; widen = sup. Guards do not refine parity, so the assume transfer is the identity.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| datatype | `parity` | 20 | datatype parity = PBot \| PEven \| POdd \| PTop |  |
| fun | `gamma_parity` | 22 | fun gamma_parity :: "parity =&gt; int set" where<br>    "gamma_parity PBot  = {}"<br>  \| "gamma_parity PEven = {n. even n}"<br>  \| "gamma_parity POdd  = {n. odd n}"<br>  \| "gamma_parity PTop  = UNIV" |  |
| fun | `parity_le` | 30 | fun parity_le :: "parity =&gt; parity =&gt; bool" where<br>    "parity_le PBot  _     = True"<br>  \| "parity_le _     PTop  = True"<br>  \| "parity_le PEven PEven = True"<br>  \| "parity_le POdd  POdd  = True"<br>  \| "parity_le _     _     = False" |  |
| definition | `less_eq_parity` | 49 | definition less_eq_parity :: "parity =&gt; parity =&gt; bool" where "(a::parity) ≤ b = parity_le a b" |  |
| definition | `less_parity` | 50 | definition less_parity    :: "parity =&gt; parity =&gt; bool" where "(a::parity) &lt;  b = (parity_le a b ∧ ¬ parity_le b a)"<br>instance ..<br>end<br><br>instance parity :: preorder<br>proof<br>  fix x y z :: parity<br>  show "(x &lt; y) = (x ≤ y ∧ ¬ y ≤ x)" unfolding less_parity_def less_eq_parity_def by simp<br>  show "x ≤ x" by (simp add: less_eq_parity_def parity_le_refl)<br>  show "x ≤ y ⟹ y ≤ z ⟹ x ≤ z" unfolding less_eq_parity_def by (rule parity_le_trans)<br>qed<br><br>instantiation parity :: bot begin |  |
| definition | `bot_parity` | 63 | definition "bot_parity = PBot"<br>instance ..<br>end |  |
| fun | `join_parity` | 69 | fun join_parity :: "parity =&gt; parity =&gt; parity" where<br>    "join_parity PBot  b     = b"<br>  \| "join_parity a     PBot  = a"<br>  \| "join_parity PTop  _     = PTop"<br>  \| "join_parity _     PTop  = PTop"<br>  \| "join_parity PEven PEven = PEven"<br>  \| "join_parity POdd  POdd  = POdd"<br>  \| "join_parity _     _     = PTop" |  |
| definition | `sup_parity` | 101 | definition sup_parity :: "parity =&gt; parity =&gt; parity" where "sup_parity = join_parity"<br>instance ..<br>end<br><br>instance parity :: semilattice_sup<br>proof<br>  fix x y z :: parity<br>  show "x ≤ x ⊔ y" unfolding sup_parity_def less_eq_parity_def by (rule join_parity_ub1)<br>  show "y ≤ x ⊔ y" unfolding sup_parity_def less_eq_parity_def by (rule join_parity_ub2)<br>  show "y ≤ x ⟹ z ≤ x ⟹ y ⊔ z ≤ x" unfolding sup_parity_def less_eq_parity_def by (rule join_parity_least)<br>qed<br><br>instance parity :: bounded_semilattice_sup_bot .. |  |
| definition | `widen` | 118 | definition "widen (a :: parity) b = join_parity a b" |  |
| definition | `narrow` | 119 | definition "narrow (a :: parity) b = a"<br>instance proof<br>  fix a b :: parity<br>  show "a ≤ widen a b" unfolding less_eq_parity_def widen_parity_def by (rule join_parity_ub1)<br>  show "b ≤ widen a b" unfolding less_eq_parity_def widen_parity_def by (rule join_parity_ub2)<br>  show "b ≤ a ⟹ b ≤ narrow a b" unfolding narrow_parity_def by simp<br>  show "b ≤ a ⟹ narrow a b ≤ a" unfolding narrow_parity_def by (simp add: less_eq_parity_def parity_le_refl)<br>qed<br>end |  |
| fun | `plus_parity` | 132 | fun plus_parity :: "parity =&gt; parity =&gt; parity" where<br>    "plus_parity PBot  _     = PBot"<br>  \| "plus_parity _     PBot  = PBot"<br>  \| "plus_parity PEven PEven = PEven"<br>  \| "plus_parity POdd  POdd  = PEven"<br>  \| "plus_parity PEven POdd  = POdd"<br>  \| "plus_parity POdd  PEven = POdd"<br>  \| "plus_parity _     _     = PTop"<br>instance ..<br>end<br><br>instantiation parity :: minus begin |  |
| fun | `minus_parity` | 144 | fun minus_parity :: "parity =&gt; parity =&gt; parity" where<br>    "minus_parity PBot  _     = PBot"<br>  \| "minus_parity _     PBot  = PBot"<br>  \| "minus_parity PEven PEven = PEven"<br>  \| "minus_parity POdd  POdd  = PEven"<br>  \| "minus_parity PEven POdd  = POdd"<br>  \| "minus_parity POdd  PEven = POdd"<br>  \| "minus_parity _     _     = PTop"<br>instance ..<br>end<br><br>instantiation parity :: times begin |  |
| fun | `times_parity` | 156 | fun times_parity :: "parity =&gt; parity =&gt; parity" where<br>    "times_parity PBot  _     = PBot"<br>  \| "times_parity _     PBot  = PBot"<br>  \| "times_parity PEven _     = PEven"<br>  \| "times_parity _     PEven = PEven"<br>  \| "times_parity POdd  POdd  = POdd"<br>  \| "times_parity _     _     = PTop"<br>instance ..<br>end |  |
| fun | `parity_of_int` | 166 | fun parity_of_int :: "int =&gt; parity" where<br>  "parity_of_int n = (if even n then PEven else POdd)" |  |
| fun | `aval_parity` | 183 | fun aval_parity :: "aexp =&gt; (vname =&gt; parity) =&gt; parity" where<br>    "aval_parity (N n)       σ = parity_of_int n"<br>  \| "aval_parity (V v)       σ = σ v"<br>  \| "aval_parity (Plus  a b) σ = aval_parity a σ + aval_parity b σ"<br>  \| "aval_parity (Minus a b) σ = aval_parity a σ - aval_parity b σ"<br>  \| "aval_parity (Times a b) σ = aval_parity a σ * aval_parity b σ" |  |
| definition | `gamma_abs_parity` | 223 | definition gamma_abs_parity [simp]: "gamma (a :: parity) = gamma_parity a"<br>instance proof<br>  show "gamma (bot :: parity) = {}" unfolding bot_parity_def by simp<br>next<br>  fix a b :: parity<br>  assume H: "a ≤ b"<br>  have "gamma_parity a ⊆ gamma_parity b" using H unfolding less_eq_parity_def by (rule gamma_parity_mono)<br>  then show "gamma a ⊆ gamma b" by simp<br>qed<br>end<br><br>instance parity :: abstract_domain ..<br><br>end |  |

| fun | `parity_tf_st` | 21 | fun parity_tf_st :: "edge_action ⇒ parity st ⇒ parity st" where<br>    "parity_tf_st EA_Nop s = s"<br>  \| "parity_tf_st (EA_Assign x a) s = update_st s x (aval_parity a (lookup_st s))"<br>  \| "parity_tf_st (EA_Assume b) s = s"<br>  \| "parity_tf_st (EA_AssumeNot b) s = s"<br>  \| "parity_tf_st (EA_Ret e _) s = (case e of<br>      None ⇒ s<br>    \| Some a ⇒ update_st s ret_var (aval_parity a (lookup_st s)))" |  |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Sign/Sign_Backward.thy

> The seven-element sign lattice has a well-defined glb. Adding `semilattice_inf` here gives *`inf_mono`* for free, which is needed for the monotonicity proof of *bfilter*.

### src/Analysis/Instances/Sign/Sign_DG.thy

> Sign is the diagonal D/G instance: answers and side effects use the same abstract-state domain. The entry transfer therefore combines the caller answer and global side effect before binding parameters. All generator and collecting results follow from the generic locale; only transfer soundness is domain-specific.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| definition | `sign_dg_gamma` | 24 | definition sign_dg_gamma ::<br>  "(pp × unit + unit ⇒ (sign abs_state, sign abs_state) dg_state)<br>   ⇒ pp ⇒ store set"<br>where<br>  "sign_dg_gamma ≡ sound_dg_spec.dg_gamma gamma_unit" | The joint concretization at a program point and the configured equation system, named natively so consumers do not reach through the locale prefix. |
| definition | `sign_dg_generator` | 30 | definition sign_dg_generator ::<br>  "cfg ⇒ sign abs_state ⇒ sign abs_state ⇒ sign abs_state<br>   ⇒ (pp × unit, unit, (sign abs_state, sign abs_state) dg_state) eqsT"<br>where<br>  "sign_dg_generator ≡ sound_dg_spec.dg_gen (unit_dg_spec sign_tf)" |  |
| locale | `sign_dg_api` | 45 | locale sign_dg_api<br><br>sublocale sign_dg_api ⊆ sound_dg_spec "unit_dg_spec sign_tf" gamma_unit<br>  rewrites "sound_dg_spec.dg_gamma gamma_unit = sign_dg_gamma"<br>       and "sound_dg_spec.dg_gen (unit_dg_spec sign_tf) = sign_dg_generator"<br>  apply (rule sound_dg_spec_unit[OF sign_is_sound_transfer])<br>   apply (simp add: sign_dg_gamma_def)<br>  apply (simp add: sign_dg_generator_def)<br>  done<br><br>context sign_dg_api<br>begin | Public DG API for the Sign domain. The sublocale interpretation rewrites facts inherited from `sound_dg_spec` into this native vocabulary, so the exported theorem below cites the generic collecting-soundness fact directly with no manual unfold/fold bridging. The trailing bare interpretation flattens the locale so consumers see the same top-level names as before; `sign_dg` above stays untouched for the existing `sign_dg.`-qualified call sites. |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Sign/Sign_Exec.thy

> Executable transport requires the finite-map transfer to commute with its abstract-state interpretation. The sign transfer proves this once, action by action; the generic bridge supplies the remaining solver transport.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| definition | `assume_sign_st` | 26 | definition assume_sign_st :: "bexp =&gt; sign st =&gt; sign st" where<br>  "assume_sign_st b s = bfilter_sign_st b True s" | `afilter_sign_st` / `bfilter_sign_st` and their commutation with `afilter_sign` / `bfilter_sign` through `fun_of_st` come from `Sign_Backward` -- the sign specialization of the generic `backward_domain` executable mirror (`Exec_Backward`). The commutation induction is proved once there, not per domain. |
| definition | `assume_not_sign_st` | 29 | definition assume_not_sign_st :: "bexp =&gt; sign st =&gt; sign st" where<br>  "assume_not_sign_st b s = bfilter_sign_st b False s" |  |
| definition | `enter_sign_st` | 42 | definition enter_sign_st :: "sign st ⇒ sign st" where<br>  "enter_sign_st = enter_frame_D_st STop" |  |
| fun | `sign_tf_st` | 52 | fun sign_tf_st :: "edge_action ⇒ sign st ⇒ sign st" where<br>    "sign_tf_st EA_Nop s = s"<br>  \| "sign_tf_st (EA_Assign x a) s = update_st s x (aval_sign a (lookup_st s))"<br>  \| "sign_tf_st (EA_Assume b) s = assume_sign_st b s"<br>  \| "sign_tf_st (EA_AssumeNot b) s = assume_not_sign_st b s"<br>  \| "sign_tf_st (EA_Ret e _) s = (case e of<br>      None ⇒ s<br>    \| Some a ⇒ update_st s ret_var (aval_sign a (lookup_st s)))" |  |

### src/Analysis/Instances/Sign/Sign_Exec_Sound.thy

> High-level vocabulary for the executable analyzer, so example statements read at the level of intent rather than solver plumbing: • `sign_exec_raw Π ps main` -- the raw solver solution (`pp + unit => sign st`), code-generating, the thing @{command value} / `eval` evaluates; • `sign_exec Π ps main` -- the analyzer's computed abstract state at the program exit (a `sign abs_state`), read back through `fun_of_st`; • `sign_exec_terminates Π ps main` -- the single assumption: the vendored solver terminates on this program. `sign_exec_sound_collecting` / `sign_exec_sound_trace` are the program-parametric soundness theorems; concrete examples only fix a program and instantiate them.

### src/Analysis/Instances/Sign/Sign_Lattice.thy

> sign abstracts integers by their sign: Bot -- empty (unreachable / undefined) Neg -- strictly negative {n \| n < 0} NonPos -- non-positive {n \| n ≤ 0} Zero -- exactly zero {0} NonNeg -- non-negative {n \| n ≥ 0} Pos -- strictly positive {n \| n > 0} Top -- all integers UNIV Seven-element lattice (Bot \<sqsubseteq> Neg,Zero,Pos \<sqsubseteq> NonPos,NonNeg \<sqsubseteq> Top). Finite; no widening needed.

| Kind | Name | Line | Definition | Docstring |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| datatype | `sign` | 23 | datatype sign = SBot \| SNeg \| SNonPos \| SZero \| SNonNeg \| SPos \| STop |  |
| fun | `gamma_sign` | 27 | fun gamma_sign :: "sign =&gt; int set" where<br>    "gamma_sign SBot    = {}"<br>  \| "gamma_sign SNeg    = {n. n &lt; 0}"<br>  \| "gamma_sign SNonPos = {n. n ≤ 0}"<br>  \| "gamma_sign SZero   = {0}"<br>  \| "gamma_sign SNonNeg = {n. n ≥ 0}"<br>  \| "gamma_sign SPos    = {n. n &gt; 0}"<br>  \| "gamma_sign STop    = UNIV" |  |
| fun | `sign_le` | 38 | fun sign_le :: "sign =&gt; sign =&gt; bool" where<br>    "sign_le SBot    _       = True"<br>  \| "sign_le _       STop    = True"<br>  \| "sign_le SNeg    SNeg    = True"<br>  \| "sign_le SNeg    SNonPos = True"<br>  \| "sign_le SNonPos SNonPos = True"<br>  \| "sign_le SZero   SZero   = True"<br>  \| "sign_le SZero   SNonPos = True"<br>  \| "sign_le SZero   SNonNeg = True"<br>  \| "sign_le SNonNeg SNonNeg = True"<br>  \| "sign_le SPos    SPos    = True"<br>  \| "sign_le SPos    SNonNeg = True"<br>  \| "sign_le _       _       = False" |  |
| definition | `less_eq_sign` | 70 | definition less_eq_sign :: "sign =&gt; sign =&gt; bool" where "(a::sign) &lt;= b = sign_le a b" |  |
| definition | `less_sign` | 71 | definition less_sign    :: "sign =&gt; sign =&gt; bool" where "(a::sign) &lt;  b = (sign_le a b ∧ ¬ sign_le b a)"<br>instance ..<br>end<br><br>instance sign :: preorder<br>proof intro_classes<br>  fix x y z :: sign<br>  show "(x &lt; y) = (x ≤ y ∧ ¬ y ≤ x)"<br>    unfolding less_sign_def less_eq_sign_def by simp<br>  show "x ≤ x"<br>    by (simp add: less_eq_sign_def sign_le_refl)<br>  show "x ≤ y ⟹ y ≤ z ⟹ x ≤ z"<br>    unfolding less_eq_sign_def by (rule sign_le_trans)<br>qed |  |
| definition | `bot_sign` | 88 | definition "bot_sign = SBot"<br>instance ..<br>end | The bottom instance lifts pointwise to abstract states and enables monotone least-upper-bound iteration. |

(8 more lines) |  |

| definition | `compiled_procedure_scope` | 213 | definition compiled_procedure_scope ::<br>  "proc_table ⇒ pname list ⇒ pname ⇒ com ⇒ cfg ⇒ pp ⇒ procedure_scope" where<br>  "compiled_procedure_scope Π ps mnm main g p =<br>    (let owner = compiled_owner_of Π ps mnm main p;<br>         decl = Π owner;<br>         fs = if owner = mnm then [] else<br>           (case decl of None ⇒ [] \| Some d ⇒ formals d);<br>         ret = if owner = mnm then None else Some ret_var;<br>         ls = filter (λx. x ∉ set fs ∧ x ≠ ret_var ∧ ¬ is_global x)<br>           (owner_assigned_vars g (compiled_owner_of Π ps mnm main) owner)<br>     in ⦇scope_formals = fs, scope_locals = ls, scope_return_slot = ret⦈)" |  |

| definition | `analysis_call_to_return_edges` | 344 | definition analysis_call_to_return_edges ::<br>  "('ctx, 'g, 'a, 'd) analysis_graph_config ⇒ cfg ⇒ (pp × 'ctx) list<br>    ⇒ (('ctx, 'g) analysis_node × analysis_edge_kind ×<br>       ('ctx, 'g) analysis_node) list" where<br>  "analysis_call_to_return_edges cfg g covered =<br>    concat (map (λsrc_ctx.<br>      concat (map (λc. case c of (call, _, entry, cont) ⇒<br>        (case entry of FunctionEntry p ⇒<br>          if fst src_ctx = call ∧ local_node_member covered cont (snd src_ctx)<br>          then [(LocalNode call (snd src_ctx), CallToReturnEdge p,<br>                 LocalNode cont (snd src_ctx))]<br>          else []<br>        \| _ ⇒ [])) (cfg_calls_list g))) covered)" | Call sites and their continuations already share a context: `cont` is the fourth component of every `calls` tuple (@{file "../../../CFG/CFG_Def.thy"}). This edge is purely presentational -- it draws that pairing directly, alongside the real interprocedural path through `EnterEdge` and `CombineEdge`, so a call site and its return-site stay visually linked even when the callee cluster sits elsewhere in the diagram. |

| definition | `build_analysis_graph_parts` | 368 | definition build_analysis_graph_parts ::<br>  "('ctx, 'g, 'a, 'd) analysis_graph_config ⇒ cfg<br>    ⇒ (pp × 'ctx) list ⇒ 'g list ⇒ (pp × 'ctx + 'g ⇒ 'a)<br>    ⇒ ('ctx, 'g) analysis_graph" where<br>  "build_analysis_graph_parts cfg g covered global_keys sol =<br>    (let locals = covered_local_nodes covered;<br>         globals = analysis_global_nodes cfg sol global_keys;<br>         sources = analysis_source_nodes cfg;<br>         ns = locals @ globals @ sources<br>     in (analysis_context_clusters cfg covered @ analysis_global_cluster ns<br>           @ analysis_source_cluster ns,<br>         ns,<br>         analysis_intra_edges g covered @ analysis_enter_edges cfg g covered sol<br>           @ analysis_combine_edges cfg g covered sol<br>           @ analysis_call_to_return_edges cfg g covered))" |  |

Solver coverage remains extensional and can be checked propositionally by clients. |
